fill="currentColor"></path></g></g></svg></a><div class="MobileNav_icons__4ORBL"><a aria-label="Payload&#x27;s GitHub" class="MobileNav_github__cBYpY" href="https://github.com/payloadcms/payload" rel="noreferrer" target="_blank"><svg height="100%" viewBox="0 0 98 96" width="100%" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path clip-rule="evenodd" d="M48.854 0C21.839 0 0 22 0 49.217c0 21.756 13.993 4.125-1.304 13.426 5.052a46.97 46.97 0 0 1 12.214-1.63c4.125 0 8.33.571 12.213 1.63 9.302-6.356 13.427-5.052 13.427-5.052 2.67 6.763.97 11.816.485 13.038 3.155 3.422 5.015 7.822 5.015 13.2 0 fill="currentColor" fill-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></a><div class="Docsearch_docSearch__tCi81"><!--$?--><template id="B:1"></template><!--/$--></div><div aria-label="Open menu" class="MobileNav_modalToggler__CQSsz"><svg fill="none" height="25" viewBox="0 0 25 25" width="25" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><rect fill="currentColor" height="2" width="18" x="3.5" y="4.5"></rect><rect fill="currentColor" height="2" width="18" x="3.5" y="11.5"></rect><rect fill="currentColor" height="2" width="18" x="3.5" y="18.5"></rect></svg></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><!--$--><!--/$--></header></div><div><div class="RenderDocs_wrap__HtZBM Gutter_leftGutter__UWO6t Gutter_rightGutter__qUzUU"><div class="grid"><div aria-hidden="true"></div><main class="cols-8 start-5 cols-m-8 start-m-1 RenderDocs_content__XnnWb"><h1 class="RenderDocs_title__h1NVD">Local API</h1><div class="RenderDocs_mdx__C8FvH"><div class="payload-richtext docs-richtext"><p>The Payload Local API gives you the ability to execute the same operations that are available through REST and GraphQL within Node, directly on your server. Here, you don&#x27;t need to deal with server latency or network speed whatsoever and can interact directly with your database.</p><div class="Banner_banner__3Dn4G banner Banner_success__09_1Z"><div class="payload-richtext"><p>  <strong>Tip:</strong></p><p>The Local API is incredibly powerful when used in React Server Components and other similar server-side contexts. With other headless CMS, you need to request your data from third-party servers via an HTTP layer, which can add significant loading time to your server-rendered pages. With Payload, you don&#x27;t have to leave your server to gather the data you need. It can be incredibly fast and is definitely a game changer.</p></div></div><p>Here are some common examples of how you can use the Local API:</p><ul class="list-bullet"><li class="" value="1">Fetching Payload data within React Server Components</li><li class="" value="2">Seeding data via Node seed scripts that you write and maintain</li><li class="" value="3">Opening custom Next.js route handlers which feature additional functionality but still rely on Payload</li><li class="" value="4">Within <a href="../access-control/overview" id="">Access Control</a> and <a href="../hooks/overview" id="">Hooks</a></li></ul><h2 class="Heading_node__Fsogy" id="accessing-payload"><a aria-label="Link to Accessing Payload" href="#accessing-payload"><svg class="Heading_linkedHeading__D2BzT icons_icon__6EVpU icons_large__tRw9w" fill="none" height="24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></a>Accessing Payload</h2><p>You can gain access to the currently running <code>payload</code> object via two ways:</p><h4 class="Heading_node__Fsogy" id="accessing-from-args-or-req"><a aria-label="Link to Accessing from args or  req" href="#accessing-from-args-or-req"><svg class="Heading_linkedHeading__D2BzT icons_icon__6EVpU icons_large__tRw9w" fill="none" height="24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></a>Accessing from args or <code>req</code></h4><p>In most places within Payload itself, you can access <code>payload</code> directly from the arguments of <a href="../hooks/overview" id="">Hooks</a>, <a href="../access-control/overview" id="">Access Control</a>, <a href="../fields/overview#validation" id="">Validation</a> functions, and similar. This is the simplest way to access Payload in most cases. Most config functions take the <code>req</code> (Request) object, which has Payload bound to it (<code>req.payload</code>).</p><p>Example:</p><div class="Code_codeWrap__cGPns Code_disableMinHeight__XTKwL lexical-code code-block-wrap notranslate" data-theme="dark" translate="no"><div class="Code_code__BTtCd code-block prism-code language-ts" style="color:#e6edf3"><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">1</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">const</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token literal-property property" style="color:#e6edf3">afterChangeHook</span><span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token plain"> CollectionAfterChangeHook </span><span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">async</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">2</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain">  </span><span class="token literal-property property" style="color:#e6edf3">req</span><span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"> payload </span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">3</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token operator">=&gt;</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">4</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain">  </span><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">const</span><span class="token plain"> posts </span><span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">await</span><span class="token plain"> payload</span><span class="token punctuation">.</span><span class="token function" style="color:#d2a8ff">find</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">5</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain">    </span><span class="token literal-property property" style="color:#e6edf3">collection</span><span class="token operator">:</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token string" style="color:#a5d6ff">&#x27;posts&#x27;</span><span class="token punctuation">,</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">6</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain">  </span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">7</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token punctuation">}</span></div></div></div></div><h4 class="Heading_node__Fsogy" id="importing-it"><a aria-label="Link to Importing it" href="#importing-it"><svg class="Heading_linkedHeading__D2BzT icons_icon__6EVpU icons_large__tRw9w" fill="none" height="24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></a>Importing it</h4><p>If you want to import Payload in places where you don&#x27;t have the option to access it from function arguments or <code>req</code>, you can import it and initialize it.</p><div class="Code_codeWrap__cGPns Code_disableMinHeight__XTKwL lexical-code code-block-wrap notranslate" data-theme="dark" translate="no"><div class="Code_code__BTtCd code-block prism-code language-ts" style="color:#e6edf3"><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">1</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">import</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"> getPayload </span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">from</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token string" style="color:#a5d6ff">&#x27;payload&#x27;</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">2</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">import</span><span class="token plain"> config </span><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">from</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token string" style="color:#a5d6ff">&#x27;@payload-config&#x27;</span><span class="token plain"></span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">3</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain" style="display:inline-block">
</span></div></div><div class="token-line Code_line__q5GJ_" style="color:#e6edf3"><span class="Code_lineNumber__XZgDa">4</span><div class="Code_lineCodeWrapper__g0OaV"><span class="token plain"></span><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">const</span><span class="token plain"> payload </span><span class="token operator">=</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token keyword" style="color:#ff7b72">await</span><span class="token plain"> </span><span class="token function" style="color:#d2a8ff">getPayload</span><span class="token punctuation">(</span><span class="token punctuation">{</span><span class="token plain"> config </span><span class="token punctuation">}</span><span class="token punctuation">)</span></div></div></div></div><p>If you&#x27;re working in Next.js&#x27; development mode, Payload will work with Hot Module Replacement (HMR), and as you make changes to your Payload Config, your usage of Payload will always be in sync with your changes. In production, <code>getPayload</code> simply disables all HMR functionality so you don&#x27;t need to write your code any differently. We handle optimization for you in production mode.</p><p>If you are accessing Payload via function arguments or <code>req.payload</code>, HMR is automatically supported if you are using it within Next.js.</p><p>For more information about using Payload outside of Next.js, <a href="./outside-nextjs" id="">click here</a>.</p><h2 class="Heading_node__Fsogy" id="local-options-available"><a aria-label="Link to Local options available" href="#local-options-available"><svg class="Heading_linkedHeading__D2BzT icons_icon__6EVpU icons_large__tRw9w" fill="none" height="24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></a>Local options available</h2><p>You can specify more options within the Local API vs. REST or GraphQL due to the server-only context that they are executed in.</p><div class="lexical-table-container"><table class="lexical-table" style="border-collapse:collapse"><tbody><tr class="lexical-table-row"><th class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-1"><p> Local Option         </p></th><th class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-1"><p> Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           </p></th></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>collection</code>         </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> Required for Collection operations. Specifies the Collection slug to operate against.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>data</code>               </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> The data to use within the operation. Required for <code>create</code>, <code>update</code>.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>depth</code>              </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <a href="../queries/depth" id="">Control auto-population</a> of nested relationship and upload fields.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>locale</code>             </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> Specify <a href="../configuration/localization" id="">locale</a> for any returned documents.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>select</code>             </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> Specify <a href="../queries/select" id="">select</a> to control which fields to include to the result.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>populate</code>           </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> Specify <a href="../queries/select#populate" id="">populate</a> to control which fields to include to the result from populated documents.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>fallbackLocale</code>     </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> Specify a <a href="../configuration/localization" id="">fallback locale</a> to use for any returned documents. This can be a single locale or array of locales.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>overrideAccess</code>     </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> Skip access control. By default, this property is set to true within all Local API operations.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>overrideLock</code>       </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> By default, document locks are ignored (<code>true</code>). Set to <code>false</code> to enforce locks and prevent operations when a document is locked by another user. <a href="../admin/locked-documents" id="">More details</a>.                                                                                                                                                                         </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>user</code>               </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> If you set <code>overrideAccess</code> to <code>false</code>, you can pass a user to use against the access control checks.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>showHiddenFields</code>   </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> Opt-in to receiving hidden fields. By default, they are hidden from returned documents in accordance to your config.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>pagination</code>         </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> Set to <code>false</code> to disable pagination calculations and document count queries. Payload still applies any positive <code>limit</code>.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>context</code>            </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <a href="../hooks/context" id="">Context</a>, which will then be passed to <code>context</code> and <code>req.context</code>, which can be read by hooks. Useful if you want to pass additional information to the hooks which shouldn&#x27;t be necessarily part of the document, for example a <code>triggerBeforeChange</code> option which can be read by the BeforeChange hook to determine if it should run or not. </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>disableErrors</code>      </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> When set to <code>true</code>, errors will not be thrown. Instead, the <code>findByID</code> operation will return <code>null</code>, and the <code>find</code> operation will return an empty documents array.                                                                                                                                                                                                   </p></td></tr><tr class="lexical-table-row"><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> <code>disableTransaction</code> </p></td><td class="lexical-table-cell lexical-table-cell-header-0"><p> When set to <code>true</code>, a <a href="../database/transactions" id="">database transactions</a> will not be initialized.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      </p></td></tr></tbody></table></div><p><em>There are more options available on an operation by operation basis outlined below.</em></p><h2 class="Heading_node__Fsogy" id="transactions"><a aria-label="Link to Transactions" href="#transactions"><svg class="Heading_linkedHeading__D2BzT icons_icon__6EVpU icons_large__tRw9w" fill="none" height="24" stroke="currentColor" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></a>Transactions</h2><p>When your database uses transactions you need to thread req through to all local operations. Postgres uses transactions and MongoDB uses transactions when you are using replica sets. Passing req without transactions is still recommended.</p><div class="Code_codeWrap__cGPns Code_disableMinHeight__XTKwL lexical-code code-block-wrap notranslate" data-theme="dark" translate="no"><div
